TL;DR: 本研究调查了软件工程师在专业开发中使用大语言模型（LLM）时的依赖、过度依赖及成瘾相关行为。结果显示，LLM主要形成了功能性依赖，并逐渐成为日常开发流程的一部分；过度依赖迹象较为明显，而成瘾相关行为相对少见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM广泛用于代码生成、调试和文档编写，软件工程师对其依赖程度及潜在不当使用模式受到关注。本研究旨在理解专业开发者如何体验和使用LLM，并区分功能性依赖、过度依赖与成瘾相关行为。

Method: 研究采用探索性问卷调查，收集119名软件从业者的数据。分析方法包括描述性统计，以及对开放式回答进行定性主题分析。

Result: 参与者主要表现出功能性依赖，原因在于LLM能够提升生产力和效率，并被整合进日常开发活动。部分参与者存在过度依赖倾向，例如优先使用LLM而非查阅文档或咨询同事，但仍会对生成结果进行验证。成瘾相关报告较少，主要涉及难以控制使用频率或对技术产生情感依附，尚未普遍表现为明显的失控。

Conclusion: LLM正在成为专业软件工程实践中的习惯性工具。总体而言，其使用主要是功能性的，但开发团队仍需通过信任校准、保持专业判断和持续验证输出，促进适度且可靠的技术依赖。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [110] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将测试场景、测试系统与被测系统作为统一分析对象，从可控性、可观测性和可隔离性三个维度评估安全关键软件的场景化验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究多聚焦单一软件或测试对象，难以系统分析场景构造、测试执行、证据整理与异常归因之间的协同关系，导致验证盲区和不可行动的测试结论。

Method: JTA通过三个分析域、三个连接桥和“分析—设计—评估—改进”闭环组织验证活动；引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲区识别及面向连接桥的设计措施，将能力缺口映射到控制点、证据组织方式和归因边界的具体改进。

Result: 以ArduPilot失效保护验证为例，链路中断场景的验证能力相对成熟；状态估计异常场景则因证据对齐不足和归因语义薄弱而更难验证。该案例说明JTA能够识别跨场景、测试系统和被测系统的联合能力缺口。

Conclusion: JTA为安全关键软件的场景化验证提供了架构化建模、设计与评估基础，可补充而非替代现有测试和安全分析技术，尤其适用于定位验证盲区并指导可执行的验证能力改进。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对开源软件项目核心贡献者流失问题，利用贡献代码的源代码度量指标评估贡献者能力，并提出用于预测任务所需能力水平的能力模型，以支持候选人筛选、培养和核心贡献者替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心贡献者的退出可能危及开源项目和社区的持续运行，而寻找替代者通常耗时且困难。既有研究虽然能够提供贡献者能力的部分线索，但对能力层级和实际解决任务所需能力的刻画不够深入，导致项目难以准确判断候选人的技术胜任度。

Method: 从贡献者提交的源代码中收集代码度量指标，用于评估其技术能力；同时构建一个能力模型，以预测完成特定任务所需的能力水平。研究还提供了包含代码、数据和文档的复现实验包。

Result: 论文提出了基于源代码度量的贡献者能力评估方法和任务能力需求预测模型。摘要未报告具体实验数据、预测性能、对比基线或统计显著性，因此无法据此判断模型的实际准确性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 对贡献者能力进行更细粒度、量化的评估，有望帮助开源项目识别具备潜力的候选人并开展针对性培养，从而缓解核心贡献者流失问题。不过，模型的有效性仍需结合完整实验结果和跨项目复现实证进一步验证。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [112] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: JDomInO是一套面向战术领域驱动设计（DDD）的双向同步工具链，通过共享元模型连接Java代码与领域模型，支持从模型生成代码以及从代码反向重建模型，以缓解两者随系统演化产生的偏离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD的共享词汇和领域模型只有在模型与实现保持一致时才能持续发挥作用。然而实际开发中，代码变更和模型更新往往不同步，导致领域知识与代码结构逐渐脱节。

Method: JDomInO基于统一元模型实现双向映射：正向路径以领域模型为输入，确定性地生成Java代码结构；反向路径分析已有Java代码并重建领域模型。研究还提出将结构化领域模型作为AI代码助手的上下文层，以提供聚合边界和DDD语义信息。

Result: 正向路径已在酒店管理场景中完成验证，覆盖元模型中的全部12种构建块类型。反向路径的映射逻辑已通过单元测试，但端到端验证仍在进行中。

Conclusion: JDomInO为保持DDD模型与Java代码的一致性提供了可行的双向同步机制。现有结果证明了正向生成能力和反向映射逻辑的初步有效性，但反向路径仍需进一步的端到端实验验证；结构化领域模型也可能提升AI代码助手对DDD约束和语义的遵循能力。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [113] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AssertMate，一种基于多智能体协作的断言生成框架，通过实际值构造、多视角期望值预测和LLM评审机制，提高LLM生成测试断言的准确性、稳定性与缺陷检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有断言生成方法存在准确率有限、过度依赖过采样以及一次性提示导致结果受模型随机性影响等问题，因此需要一种更可靠、可扩展且能综合多种推理视角的生成方法。

Method: AssertMate包含三个核心组件：首先利用静态分析和类型感知启发式方法定位断言目标并构造实际值；其次通过代码生成、检索增强生成（RAG）和思维链（CoT）推理代理，从多个视角预测期望值；最后采用LLM作为评审器，对候选断言进行协作筛选。该框架还与EvoSuite集成，用于验证实际测试生成效果。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上的实验表明，AssertMate在编译成功率、测试通过率和缺陷检测能力方面显著优于现有先进方法。与EvoSuite集成后，其变异覆盖率和变异体杀死数量也更高。消融实验显示，三个组件均能带来显著且互补的性能提升。

Conclusion: 聚合代码生成、检索增强、思维链推理及LLM评审等多种视角，能够有效提升LLM断言生成的质量、可靠性和实用性。AssertMate验证了基于智能体协作的断言生成框架在自动化软件测试中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [114] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeGrep，一个通过多轮并行grep、glob和文件读取进行代码检索的14B检索智能体，用于减少LLM编码智能体在定位待修改文件上的开销。在SWE-Bench Verified上，CodeGrep在保持修复率的同时，将已解决任务的交互轮数减少15%、Token消耗减少19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码智能体在真正修改代码前，往往将大量轮次和Token用于仓库探索与文件定位，导致推理成本高、效率低。研究目标是将文件检索从下游编码智能体中分离出来，并验证高质量检索是否能降低整体修复成本。

Method: 训练一个14B的CodeGrep检索智能体，使用GRPO进行端到端强化学习，使其能够多轮并行调用grep、glob和read工具并返回候选文件；下游编码智能体保持冻结。研究利用67K条开源智能体轨迹，通过CATM挖掘监督数据，并构建支持多轮智能体强化学习的Git worktree环境。同时比较BM25、Jina与CodeGrep，并分析将效率信号施加于优势层而非奖励层的影响。

Result: 在全部500个SWE-Bench Verified实例上，CodeGrep的修复率为27.0%，高于无检索基线的25.8%，且已解决实例的轮数减少15%、Token消耗减少19%。下游效用与检索精确率存在阈值关系：BM25精确率0.375会降低性能，Jina精确率0.445基本中性，而CodeGrep精确率0.677能够跨过降低rollout成本的有效阈值。将效率信号施加于优势层可减少KL漂移，并更稳定地转化为下游效率提升。

Conclusion: 高精度、专门化的代码检索智能体能够在不牺牲任务修复率的情况下显著降低LLM编码代理的推理成本。研究表明，检索质量必须达到一定精确率阈值，低质量检索可能反而增加负担；同时，优势层效率优化和专用训练环境有助于实现稳定收益。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [115] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了23门明确涵盖生成式人工智能软件工程内容的高年级学分课程，系统归纳其学习目标、考核方式、教学主题及所使用的AI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变专业软件开发流程，高校亟需设计相应课程。通过分析现有课程大纲，可了解这一新兴课程领域的形成方式，并为未来课程设计提供实证依据。

Method: 研究者收集并筛选23份公开可得的课程大纲和课程材料，要求课程为高年级、学分制课程，且明确涉及生成式AI与软件工程。随后采用迭代式定性编码，分析课程的学习目标、评估方式、教学主题和AI工具。

Result: 研究发现，各课程在学习目标、考核设计、教学内容和AI工具使用方面既存在共同模式，也存在明显差异。这些特征为理解生成式AI软件工程教育的当前形态提供了结构化证据。

Conclusion: 生成式AI软件工程课程已形成若干共同关注点，但整体设计仍具有较大多样性。研究结果可支持教育者改进课程开发，也为后续研究比较和构建相关课程体系提供基础。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [116] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，基于执行成功率的迭代自修复可能陷入“自修复陷阱”，生成易满足但难以暴露缺陷的回归断言；为此提出非迭代框架DCAware，通过高质量上下文建模提升测试预言机效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回归预言机方法常以执行成功作为优化目标，但该指标只是代理目标，可能与真正的缺陷揭示能力不一致。反复利用执行反馈进行修复会使模型逐渐偏向更容易满足、但检测故障能力更弱的断言，从而造成反馈驱动的性能退化。

Method: DCAware采用计算成本较低的非迭代生成流程，优先提升上下文的信噪比。该方法结合结构化静态上下文与选择性检索的动态程序状态，为LLM提供更精确、稳健的生成依据，并避免多轮自修复及其反馈偏差。

Result: 基于执行测试和变异测试的实验表明，DCAware在保持较高执行成功率的同时，持续提升了缺陷揭示能力；相较既有方法，其整体性能更优，且计算成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 在回归预言机生成任务中，提升上下文质量比增加迭代式修复复杂度更有效。执行成功率不应被视为唯一优化目标，变异测试等能够直接衡量缺陷揭示能力的指标同样应纳入方法设计与评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [117] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Git提交记录建模开发者专业能力的Web应用，用于将开发者自动分配到新项目中最匹配的长期组件或任务。实验表明，72.4%的任务可在前10名候选开发者中找到目标开发者，服务端缓存使最差情况下的速度提升9.86倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务分配研究主要针对特定类型的短期工作，对新项目中长期、专业领域组件的开发者分配关注不足。错误匹配会导致任务完成变慢，并降低代码的可维护性，因此需要一种能够利用历史开发行为进行能力建模和自动匹配的方法。

Method: 系统通过分析开发者过去的Git提交记录，建立其专业能力模型，并开发Web应用，将开发者自动分配到新项目中最合适的任务或组件。研究还比较了不同后端模型的处理速度，并评估服务端数据缓存的性能影响。

Result: 系统性能受后端模型影响，服务端数据缓存使最差情况下的处理速度提升9.86倍。在总计47名候选开发者中，72.4%的任务能够在排名前10的候选名单中找到目标开发者。

Conclusion: 基于Git提交历史的开发者专业能力建模能够支持新项目中的自动化任务分配，并表现出一定的推荐准确性。缓存机制显著改善了系统性能，但排名前10覆盖率仍表明开发者专长建模和任务匹配方法具有进一步优化空间。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [118] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了经任务适配的开源指令微调大语言模型，能否基于传感器行为的文本描述，在汽车硬件在环验证中进行数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断。结果显示，最小模型达到81.6%的准确率，与最大模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件在环验证会产生海量多变量传感器记录。传统阈值工具只能指出偏差，无法识别故障性质或定位来源；数据驱动分类器虽可能准确，却依赖大量标注数据且决策不透明，难以满足ISO 26262对可追溯性的要求。因此，研究探索兼具数据效率和解释能力的LLM诊断方案。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：首先在dSPACE实时平台上自动检查功能安全需求，筛选出违反要求的记录；随后对这些记录进行滑动窗口分析，将信号压缩为统计、关系和上下文描述，并嵌入固定提示词，由采用4位量化低秩适配的LLM输出故障位置及文字化理由。研究在汽油机案例上比较了四个参数规模为2至8 billion的模型，覆盖六类注入故障。

Result: 最小模型取得81.6%的诊断准确率，与最大模型表现相当；一个参数规模相近的模型则未能收敛。结果表明，任务适配后的诊断能力主要取决于是否成功收敛，而非参数量大小。完整的适配与评估流程可在单个普通加速器上完成。

Conclusion: 开源LLM有望作为汽车软件验证环节中的低标注需求、可解释故障诊断组件。研究同时表明，模型收敛性和任务适配策略比单纯扩大参数规模更关键。不过，结论目前基于单一汽油机案例和六类故障，仍需在更多平台、工况及安全需求上验证其泛化性和标准符合性。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [119] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentExecutor，一种面向不完整代码片段执行的多智能体框架，通过环境准备、动态探索与迭代优化、前缀程序合成三个阶段，提高代码覆盖率并降低执行时间与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意代码片段通常缺少运行上下文、依赖和资源配置，导致部分代码难以直接执行。现有方法如LExecutor和Treefix存在动作空间与反馈有限、优化策略僵化等问题，影响执行效果和效率。

Method: AgentExecutor利用多个大语言模型智能体进行思考、行动和反馈交互。框架首先准备执行环境，随后开展动态探索和迭代修正，并通过程序合成实现前缀演化。同时采用覆盖率引导的上下文裁剪和自适应优化策略，支持创建资源文件、解决环境配置等更丰富的操作。

Result: 在Stack Overflow代码片段和开源项目代码两个数据集上，AgentExecutor最高获得94%和90%的代码覆盖率，较Treefix分别提升19.9%和13.8%；执行时间最多降低80.3%，成本最多降低56.6%。

Conclusion: AgentExecutor能够有效扩展部分代码执行的动作空间，并通过反馈驱动探索和程序合成提升执行质量，在覆盖率、效率和成本方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [120] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LangChoiceBench，用于系统评估大语言模型在项目级代码生成中对Python的偏好、推荐与实现的一致性及编程语言多样性。实验显示，多数模型明显过度选择Python，且其选择常缺乏对项目需求的充分分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已观察到大语言模型在代码生成时偏向Python，但缺少可用于新模型、项目级场景的标准化测量方法。尤其在Python并非合适默认选择的软件项目中，需要评估模型能否依据实际需求进行语言决策，并保持建议与最终代码实现一致。

Method: 研究者构建LangChoiceBench，覆盖七个软件领域中的28个项目，这些任务均包含Python通常不是最佳默认方案的情形。基准从Python偏好程度、语言推荐与代码实现的一致性、输出语言多样性三个维度评估25个不同类型的大语言模型；此外，研究分析了9,826条模型推理轨迹，以识别语言选择背后的决策模式与错误类型。

Result: 结果表明，Python在模型输出中仍被严重过度选择；模型先前推荐的语言与后续实现代码之间的一致性较低。较小规模的开放权重模型通常表现出更强的Python偏好和更低的语言多样性。推理轨迹分析发现，大部分Python选择属于自动化偏好，或主要以“易用性”为理由，而非基于项目约束、性能需求、生态兼容性或部署环境进行综合判断。少数但重要的案例中，模型会虚构支持Python选择的上下文依据，即“幻象证据”，或者生成与自身推理中所选语言相矛盾的代码。

Conclusion: LangChoiceBench为评估代码生成模型的语言选择能力提供了项目级基准，并揭示了当前模型在技术决策方面的显著局限：过度依赖Python默认偏好、缺乏需求驱动的语言分析、推荐与实现脱节，以及可能产生虚构依据。未来代码模型的改进应强化对项目约束的显式推理、跨语言能力平衡和建议—实现一致性验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [121] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主软件密集型系统缺乏统一可解释性定义与开发指南的问题，整合现有定义并提出统一术语、结构化需求及解释质量概念，为标准制定、认证和审计提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统的复杂性、应用范围和承担的责任不断增加，使透明性与可解释性成为关键质量属性。然而，现有研究尚未形成普适的定义、分类体系和开发指导，难以支持系统化设计、标准化评估与认证审计。

Method: 研究首先分析并整合已有的可解释性定义，构建统一定义体系；随后提出构建自解释系统所需的结构化可解释性需求，并对形成的分类法进行分析。在此基础上，将解释良好性纳入统一定义，用于体现解释的正确性与质量。

Result: 研究形成了统一的可解释性定义和分类体系，明确了自解释系统的结构化需求，并提出将解释良好性及解释正确性作为可解释性的重要组成部分。该体系能够支持后续正式标准的制定。

Conclusion: 统一的可解释性术语和需求分类有助于规范自主及软件密集型系统的设计与评估，并为可解释系统的标准化、认证和审计奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [122] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DCAS，用于解耦CLI软件工程智能体的脚手架与后端模型，并证明将规划能力从固定脚手架迁移到模型本身，可显著提升跨脚手架泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放生态中的训练轨迹几乎都由OpenHands收集，导致模型在训练脚手架上表现良好，但部署到其他脚手架时性能明显下降。该现象主要由微调引入，并与脚手架特有的规划约定有关，因此需要研究并消除这种脚手架依赖。

Method: 提出Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding（DCAS），通过后端替换拦截层，在不修改CLI脚手架的情况下连接任意脚手架与后端模型，实现跨脚手架评测和规划感知轨迹采集。研究区分显式规划（执行前生成的独立计划）与隐式规划（贯穿智能体循环的结构性执行约定），并通过计划来源干预进行受控实验。

Result: 实验表明，规划质量是影响跨脚手架性能的高杠杆因素，其带来的收益超过观察到的跨脚手架性能下降。仅使用单一脚手架下少量DCAS规划感知轨迹进行微调，即可使模型在多个非训练脚手架上获得稳定提升；同时，显式规划与隐式规划在训练数据中的作用可以经验性区分。

Conclusion: CLI智能体的跨脚手架性能差距并非单纯源于模型能力不足，而主要来自脚手架特定的规划结构。通过DCAS收集并学习规划能力，可将规划从固定脚手架工件转化为模型内在能力，从而提升跨环境泛化性。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出全局化技能演化框架GSE，通过技能关系图、聚类式技能整合和基于回放的验证，提升LLM智能体技能的兼容性、泛化性与稳定性。在测试生成和误报过滤任务上，GSE在多个智能体及工业部署中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能演化方法通常以局部更新为主，忽视技能之间的依赖与协同关系，容易产生过拟合、任务迁移能力弱以及行为退化等问题。因此，需要一种能够全局协调技能关系并验证技能泛化性的演化机制。

Method: GSE包含三个核心组件：1）维护并共同演化技能关系图（SRG），显式建模技能间的兼容性与关联；2）采用基于聚类的技能整合，从局部更新中抽象可复用能力；3）利用基于回放的验证机制检查历史行为，抑制过拟合并避免性能回退。实验覆盖漏洞揭示测试生成和误报缺陷报告过滤两类软件工程任务，并在OpenHands、mini-SWE-agent及一个工业智能体上进行评估。

Result: 在两类任务和两种先进编码智能体上，GSE均取得最佳精确率、召回率和F1值。相较现有技能演化方法，测试生成任务的精确率提升6.1%~34.1%，召回率提升31.8%~180.0%；误报过滤任务的精确率提升15.4%~96.4%，召回率提升13.1%~19.8%。在内部工业智能体部署中，F1值进一步提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局建模技能关系、提炼可复用能力并进行回放验证，有效缓解了技能演化中的不一致、过拟合和行为回归问题。结果表明，该方法不仅在基准任务上具有稳定优势，也具备较好的跨智能体泛化能力和工业应用潜力。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [124] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用PLS-SEM分析敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素，识别出少数对项目成功具有显著影响的因素，并构建了项目成功评估模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目通常优于传统软件开发项目，但整体成功率仍然偏低，许多项目仍被认定为受挑战或失败。研究旨在弥补敏捷项目未能依据关键成功因素和现代项目成功标准进行系统评估的问题。

Method: 研究采用实证主义方法，以敏捷实践者为调查对象，使用SmartPLS实施偏最小二乘结构方程模型（PLS-SEM），检验各构念之间的关系及其对项目成功标准的影响。

Result: 研究发现，只有少数关键因素对敏捷项目成功具有显著贡献，并验证了相关构念与项目成功标准之间的显著关系。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个新的敏捷项目成功评估与测量模型，可用于识别关键成功因素并支持项目绩效评价。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [125] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估六种通用大语言模型能否通过少样本提示，将非结构化自然语言需求直接转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式。基于15条结构多样的需求和450个候选公式，结果表明，未经专门微调的LLM已具备具有实际意义的自动形式化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求通常存在歧义，而安全关键和任务关键系统的验证依赖数学上精确的形式规格。人工将非结构化需求转换为LTL公式成本高、易出错，因此需要评估通用LLM能否辅助弥合自然语言需求与形式化规格之间的差距。

Method: 研究测试六种现代商用或开源LLM，采用少样本提示处理包含15条结构差异明显需求的异构基准。对每个需求—模型组合独立生成五次，共获得450个候选LTL公式，并通过人工语义评估、pass@k（k=1、3、5）以及衡量随机生成结果语法可复现性的自一致性指标进行分析。同时，研究为生成公式配套自然语言解释，并讨论基于时间线的LTL可视化对非专家理解的辅助作用。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前通用LLM在非结构化自然语言到LTL转换任务上已达到具有实践价值的表现，且不依赖任务专用微调。多次采样、pass@k和自一致性分析揭示了模型在正确性与生成稳定性方面的表现；自然语言解释和可视化能够进一步提升公式的可理解性。

Conclusion: 现代LLM可作为半自动形式化流程中的前端辅助工具，帮助需求工程师从自然语言需求初步生成LTL规格。但其输出仍应经过人工语义审查和形式化验证，尤其适用于安全关键场景中的人机协同工作流。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Post-Hoc Trajectory-Risk Certification for Modular LLM-Based Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05199)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究多阶段自主安全代理的轨迹级置信覆盖问题，指出逐阶段校准不能自动推出全链路保证，并提出基于生成树的有效依赖建模方法及审计样本复杂度界限。实验表明，充分审计时直接评估轨迹失败率优于Bonferroni校正，但样本不足会导致其表现变差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段系统通常先进行流量分类，再进行技术归因；即使每个阶段都具有有限样本覆盖保证，阶段间依赖仍可能使整条处理轨迹失去可靠的覆盖率保证。传统Bonferroni方法虽分布无关但较保守，而相关性估计又受到审计样本量和标签选择伪相关的影响。

Method: 首先分析多阶段覆盖保证的组合问题，证明将二阶段两两相关性界直接扩展到三阶段及以上会得到下界而非所需的上界。随后提出基于阶段依赖生成树的有效替代方案，并区分真实阶段依赖与审计样本是否足以认证该依赖，推导相应的上界和信息论下界样本复杂度。实验采用两个数据集和六个开放式大语言模型，结合粗到细标签选择校正、直接轨迹失败审计、模块化证书、同模型跨模型及置换配对测试进行验证。

Result: 粗到细标签选择会制造接近完美的测量相关性；去除该伪影后，两阶段相关性从接近1降至0至0.78。审计样本达到要求后，直接轨迹失败审计相较Bonferroni紧13.7%；样本不足时则更差。仅使用逐阶段证书和两两重叠界的模块化证书平均提升0.6%。残余依赖主要来自样本难度共享，而非模型表示共享。12种配置下平均轨迹覆盖率为92.7%±2.4%（α=0.10）。跨数据集部署时，单步失覆盖率可达100%，尽管准确率仍为78%。

Conclusion: 多阶段置信保证必须在轨迹层面显式处理阶段依赖，不能简单叠加独立校准结果。生成树界提供了有效且可组合的依赖证书，但其实际收益取决于足够大的审计样本和对标签伪相关的控制。跨分布部署会显著破坏校准置信度，因此仅依赖准确率不足以评估安全代理的可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous security agents operate as staged pipelines, such as classifying network traffic and then attributing attacks to a specific technique. Split conformal prediction gives each stage finite-sample coverage, but deployment requires a trajectory-level guarantee across the full chain. These guarantees do not compose automatically when stages are independently trained and calibrated. Bonferroni allocation is distribution-free but conservative under correlated errors. We show that a natural pairwise-correlation extension to three or more stages is invalid because it gives a lower rather than an upper bound, and derive a valid spanning-tree alternative. We distinguish whether stages are dependent from whether an audit sample is large enough to certify that dependence, and give matching upper and information-theoretic lower sample-complexity bounds. We also show that coarse-to-fine label selection can create near-perfect measured correlation without learned dependence.
  On a two-stage intrusion-detection pipeline across 6 open LLMs and 2 datasets, removing this artifact reduces measured correlation from near 1 to 0-0.78. A direct audit of trajectory failure becomes 13.7% tighter than Bonferroni once the audit reaches the required sample size, but is worse when undersized. A modular certificate using per-stage certificates and a pairwise overlap bound yields a positive average gain of 0.6%, quantifying the cost of lacking joint access. Same-model, cross-model, and permuted-pairing tests show that residual dependence reflects shared sample difficulty, not shared model representations. Average trajectory coverage across 12 configurations is 92.7% +/- 2.4% at alpha = 0.10. Under cross-dataset deployment, single-step miscoverage reaches 100% even when accuracy remains 78%, showing that distribution shift destroys calibrated confidence before raw accuracy.

</details>


### [127] [ASTELD: A Six-Axis Classification Framework for Autonomous AI Agents - Design, Evaluation, and an OpenClaw Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05201)
*Siyuan Li,Peng Shu,Churan Yu,Peilong Wang,Ruidong Zhang,Bowen Guo,Xinliang Li,Ruiyu Yan,Arif Hassan Zidan,Yi Pan,Wei Ruan,Lifeng Chen,Junhao Chen,Zhaojun Ding,Yiwei Li,Zhengliang Liu,Haixing Dai,Lin Zhao,Yu Bao,Xiang Li,Wei Zhang,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASTELD六轴分类框架，从架构模式、安全态势、工具集成、执行范式、自主性与人类控制、部署拓扑六个维度系统比较自主AI代理平台。对八个平台、50多个OpenClaw衍生系统及案例证据的分析表明，该框架能够区分平台设计、解释生态分化并识别尚未占据的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI代理平台在架构、安全、工具、执行、自主性和部署方面差异显著，但缺乏统一、可复现且基于可观察属性的分类体系，导致平台比较、框架选择和未来实证研究缺少共同标准。

Method: 研究综合既有代理分类法与平台可观察属性，建立包含六个轴向及明确类别判定规则的ASTELD框架。研究者将八个代表性框架映射到该体系，利用OpenClaw开展深入案例研究，并进一步分析50多个衍生系统、五项机构评估、漏洞类别、采用情况与治理问题，以检验框架的区分能力和解释能力。

Result: ASTELD在主导配置下区分了全部八个平台，并发现三项跨平台规律：安全性与可访问性存在对角关系，执行范式与架构模式高度耦合，平台能力趋同但架构仍保持差异。OpenClaw衍生生态的创新主要集中在安全、执行和部署轴上。案例研究还形成了六类漏洞分类，并将平台坐标与实际风险、采用和治理现象联系起来。所有评估系统均未同时具备本地优先部署与企业级安全能力。

Conclusion: ASTELD为自主AI代理平台提供了可复现、可比较的操作性分析方法，可用于识别设计空白、指导平台和框架选择，并组织后续经验研究。其最重要的启示是，本地化部署与企业级安全之间仍存在明显未占据区域，同时安全、执行和部署是生态分化与未来创新的关键维度。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agent platforms differ substantially in architecture, security, tool integration, execution, autonomy, and deployment, yet the field lacks a common classification scheme for comparing these design choices. We propose ASTELD, an operational six-axis classification framework for autonomous AI agents: Architecture pattern, Security posture, Tool integration model, Execution paradigm, Level of autonomy and human control, and Deployment topology. ASTELD is constructed by synthesizing prior agent taxonomies with observable platform properties and explicit category-assignment rules. We evaluate its discriminative and explanatory utility by mapping eight representative frameworks and by using OpenClaw as an in-depth case study. The resulting profiles separate all eight platforms under their dominant configurations and reveal three cross-platform patterns: a security-accessibility diagonal, strong execution-architecture coupling, and capability convergence with persistent architectural differentiation. We further classify 50+ OpenClaw derivatives and find that innovation concentrates on the Security, Execution, and Deployment axes, indicating that ASTELD can explain where ecosystem fragmentation occurs. The OpenClaw case study also supplies a six-category vulnerability taxonomy, evidence from five institutional assessments, and adoption and governance analyses that connect platform coordinates to observed risks. These results position ASTELD as a reproducible method for comparing agent platforms, identifying unoccupied design regions, guiding framework selection, and organizing future empirical research. The analysis also exposes a consequential empty region: none of the evaluated systems combines local-first deployment with enterprise-grade security.

</details>


### [128] [A Survey of Adversarial Efficiency Degradation for Vision Transformer by Exploiting Input-adaptive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05217)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述针对视觉Transformer输入自适应推理机制的效率退化攻击，比较SlowFormer与DeSparsify在A-ViT、ATS和AdaViT上的影响，并评估其防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token pruning和early halting虽可降低计算量与能耗，但也暴露出可被攻击者操纵的计算效率攻击面。此类攻击可能在基本不降低准确率的情况下增加推理成本，对移动端和嵌入式低功耗部署尤其构成风险，因此需要系统评估攻击脆弱性及轻量级防御方法。

Method: 统一比较两类攻击：基于通用对抗补丁的SlowFormer，以及针对单幅图像生成扰动的DeSparsify。实验覆盖A-ViT、ATS和AdaViT三种token pruning框架，并使用GFLOPs、准确率损失和Attack Success（AS，表示攻击夺回模型计算节省比例）进行标准化评估。分析重点包括攻击面形成机制、不同优化策略的脆弱性及现有防御措施。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数值，但研究旨在揭示两类攻击均可通过干扰token保留或提前停止决策，显著削弱输入自适应计算节省，同时尽量保持模型准确率。不同剪枝框架的易受攻击程度可能存在差异，现有防御能否在攻击下恢复效率是重点评估对象。

Conclusion: 输入自适应推理在提升效率的同时引入了可被利用的计算攻击面。评估此类风险时，应同时关注准确率与计算开销，而非仅考察模型性能。未来防御需要在有效恢复计算节省的同时保持轻量化，以适应资源受限设备的部署需求。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) increasingly rely on input-adaptive inference, such as token pruning and early halting, to meet energy and latency budgets. This survey examines a recent class of adversarial efficiency degradation attacks that target these mechanisms to increase computation without necessarily degrading accuracy. We unify and compare two representative attacks, SlowFormer (a universal adversarial patch) and DeSparsify (per-image perturbations), across three popular token-pruning frameworks: A-ViT, ATS, and AdaViT. We standardize reporting using GFLOPs, accuracy loss, and an Attack Success (AS) metric that measures how much of the model's compute savings the attack takes away. Understanding these attacks is crucial for designing countermeasures that not only mitigate risk but also remain lightweight, since deployment often occurs in low-power settings such as mobile or embedded devices. To organize our analysis, we focus on three questions: how input-adaptive optimizations (e.g., token pruning and early halting) create attack surfaces for efficiency degradation; how such attacks operate in practice and which optimizations are most vulnerable; and which defenses exist today and whether they meaningfully restore efficiency under attack.

</details>


### [129] [CyberBridge: Bridging the Gap Between Cybersecurity Education and Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05231)
*Arthur Nijdam,Paul Stankovski Wagner,Sara Ramezanian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberBridge是一个可解释的网络安全岗位映射框架，利用Sentence-BERT将职位描述拆解为知识、技能和任务（KST）要素，并匹配到最相似的职业角色，以支持岗位推荐、市场分析和课程规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全领域发展迅速，教育项目和课程难以及时对齐行业岗位所需能力。现有岗位匹配方法往往缺乏透明度，难以说明推荐结果背后的具体能力依据，因此需要一种兼具语义匹配能力与可解释性的工具。

Method: 框架首先从网络安全职位描述中提取知识、技能和任务（KST）陈述，然后使用Sentence-BERT生成语义嵌入，并将这些要素与专业角色画像进行相似度匹配。系统进一步保留驱动匹配的具体能力信息，为推荐结果提供可追溯、可读的解释。

Result: CyberBridge支持三类应用：根据学生已完成的课程推荐相关职位和职业角色；帮助教育者及课程开发者分析市场中的岗位需求；评估不同课程项目对热门网络安全职业的准备程度。摘要未报告定量实验结果、基准比较或具体性能指标。

Conclusion: CyberBridge为网络安全教育提供了一个面向实践的、可解释的岗位—职业角色映射工具，有助于职业指导、劳动力市场分析和基于证据的课程设计。但其实际有效性仍需通过更大规模数据、专家评估和定量对比实验进一步验证。

Abstract: This full research paper presents CyberBridge, a framework that automatically maps cybersecurity job descrip- tions to professional role profiles. As the cybersecurity landscape evolves rapidly, it is difficult for academic programs to align curricula with the competencies expected in practice.
  CyberBridge addresses this gap by decomposing a given vacancy description into its constituent Knowledge, Skill, and Task (KST) statements, embedding them using a sentence- BERT model, and matching them to the most semantically similar workforce profiles. A key contribution of our approach is its interpretability. Rather than functioning as a black box, CyberBridge enables users to trace recommendations back to the specific competencies driving each match, providing a human- readable justification for the resulting mappings.
  CyberBridge supports three primary use cases; (1) job recom- mendation, providing students with vacancies and closely match- ing professional roles based on their completed curriculum, (2) market analysis, enabling educators and curriculum developers to analyze which roles are currently in demand, and (3) curriculum planning, assessing which course program best prepares students for in-demand cybersecurity roles.
  As such, CyberBridge can be used by educational institutions as a practical tool for career guidance and evidence-based curriculum development in cybersecurity education.

</details>


### [130] [The Hidden Life of Public Safety Communications Signals: A Comparative Security Analysis of TETRA, TETRAPOL, and P25](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05247)
*Larry Hernandez,Sergey Bratus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析了P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL集群式陆地移动无线电系统的信令面泄露问题，指出即使通信内容加密完善，被动监听者仍可从明文信令推断网络、地理、用户、组织及密钥管理信息，甚至在TETRAPOL特定场景下恢复未加密语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分析主要关注内容面漏洞，如密钥恢复或偶发明文，而对信令面暴露及其导致的通信关系、身份和行动模式泄露关注不足。论文旨在评估内容加密无法解决的元数据机密性风险。

Method: 研究者针对P25、TETRA和TETRAPOL标准，使用被动、只接收的软件无线电观察信令，并通过时序、关联关系、跨小区及跨群组活动等元数据进行推断；同时分析TETRAPOL紧急呼叫处理中的标准级保密缺陷，并提出身份保护和信令防护措施。

Result: 被动观察可推断网络拓扑与地理范围、终端存在状态、跨小区和群组移动、组织结构、特殊密钥域及密钥周期轮换等运营安全信息。通过跨区域信令关联，局部监听可能扩展为大范围网络映射，并削弱基于时序和关联的身份隐藏机制；TETRAPOL案例中，信令推断结合紧急呼叫处理缺陷可导致未加密语音提取。

Conclusion: LMR系统的机密性不能仅依赖流量内容加密，还必须保护信令元数据及通信关系。标准和实现应重点加强跨小区、基站及终端身份保护，减少可关联的明文信令，并修复紧急呼叫等特殊流程中的机密性缺陷。

Abstract: Public-safety agencies and critical infrastructure operators rely on trunked land-mobile radio (LMR) systems, based on P25, TETRA, and TETRAPOL. These systems are expected to protect not just the content of a communication but the fact of it. Yet LMR standards leave a stark gap between confidentiality of \emph{content} and of \emph{communication}: underneath an encrypted traffic plane, their signaling plane is almost entirely in the clear. We probe the depth and impact of adversarial inference from this exposed signaling.
  Prior security analyses of these systems have concentrated on the content plane---recovering encryption keys or capturing accidental cleartext. We show that comparably sensitive information can be \emph{inferred from passively observed signaling even if the content encryption were perfect}.
  In particular, we show that across the trunked LMR standards, a passive, receive-only software-defined radio (SDR) observer can recover operationally sensitive network topology and geography details, unit presence, mobility across cells and groups, organizational structure, as well as operational security details such as special key domains and key-epoch rotation. This signaling-plane inference reaches far beyond the observer's direct area of reception, turning \emph{local} sniffing into \emph{nationwide} network mapping capabilities that degrade or defeat LMR standards' identity obfuscation through timing and association. In the case of TETRAPOL, we demonstrate how inference and tracking of such signaling metadata and a standards-level confidentiality failure in emergency call handling enable unencrypted voice extraction.
  Finally, we discuss potential countermeasures and mitigations, including specific recommendations for protecting inter-cell, base station and subscriber identities.

</details>


### [131] [eMicro: Real-Time Multi-Hop Access Control for Microservices with eBPF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05300)
*Rizky Ramadhana Putra,Osama Bajaber,Saimon Amanuel Tsegai,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo,Yuede Ji,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出eMicro，一种面向微服务调用路径的访问控制系统，用于阻止单跳请求均合法、但整体多跳路径违反安全策略的攻击。其核心是将调用历史编码为DFA状态，并通过eBPF在内核中透明追踪和传播请求标签，实现低开销实时策略执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微服务访问控制通常仅验证相邻服务间的请求权限，无法识别跨多个服务的组合式违规路径。攻击者可能利用多个看似合法的服务调用逐步获得未授权访问或造成数据泄露，因此需要能够理解并约束完整调用链的路径感知访问控制机制。

Method: eMicro采用三项技术：（1）扩展基于历史的访问控制模型，将服务调用序列纳入授权决策；（2）将路径安全策略编译为确定有限自动机（DFA），以当前自动机状态和下一跳服务共同决定是否允许访问，从而支持常数时间查询及紧凑标签传播；（3）利用eBPF在内核态进行请求追踪和策略执行，无需修改应用代码。请求在服务间传播时携带代表历史路径的标签，系统据此更新DFA状态并验证后续调用是否合法。

Result: 作者在DeathStarBench以及来自Uber、Alibaba和ByteDance的生产云请求轨迹上评估系统，覆盖1,200万条请求工作流和数千个服务。结果表明，eMicro单次策略检查约为1微秒；可用100 MB存储5,000万条策略；相比基线方案，标签传播开销降低90%，同时对运行时性能影响可忽略。

Conclusion: eMicro通过将多跳调用历史纳入访问控制，并结合DFA状态机与eBPF内核态实现，在可扩展性、存储效率和运行时开销之间取得平衡。该方案表明路径级安全策略可在大规模微服务环境中实际部署，并能弥补传统点对点访问控制无法防御多跳攻击的缺陷。

Abstract: Modern cloud applications often comprise thousands of microservices whose interactions form complex request paths. Traditional inter-service access control restricts individual service-to-service requests, but fails to prevent multi-hop attacks, where each hop appears legitimate yet the overall path violates security intent. This gap leaves systems exposed to unauthorized access and data exfiltration. In this paper, we present eMicro, a path-aware defense system for microservices that prevents such attacks while remaining efficient and deployable. eMicro enforces real-time multi-hop access control through three key techniques: (1) history-based access control extended to capture service invocation sequences; (2) security policies encoded as efficient deterministic finite automaton (DFA), supporting constant-time lookups and compact label propagation; (3) eBPF-based in-kernel request tracing for transparent, low-overhead enforcement without code changes. Evaluations on DeathStarBench and production cloud traces from Uber, Alibaba, and ByteDance, covering 12 million request workflows and thousands of services, demonstrate the scalability of eMicro. eMicro performs policy checks in 1 microsecond, stores 50 million policies in only 100 MB, and reduces propagation overhead by 90% with negligible runtime impact. These results show that eMicro delivers scalable and efficient protection against multi-hop attacks, making it practical for deployment in large-scale microservice environments.

</details>


### [132] [Game Hopping in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06261)
*Stefan Dziembowski,Grzegorz Fabiański,Daniele Micciancio,Rafał Stefański*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HOPSCOTCH 是一个基于 Lean 4 的计算可 sound、游戏跳转式密码学证明框架，支持状态化概率预言机、形式化证明对象与优势界推导，并验证了多项经典密码学安全性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码学安全证明通常依赖手工推理，难以保证计算可靠性、复用证明并进行自动检查。HOPSCOTCH 旨在将标准的游戏跳转证明系统化、机械化，同时保留与 Lean 及 Mathlib 数学库的直接集成能力。

Method: 框架采用浅嵌入，将预言机和归约表示为普通 Lean 定义；安全性通过状态化概率预言机之间的不可区分性表达。游戏跳转被编码为显式形式对象，其构造子对应替换、归约和等价变换等标准证明步骤。框架进一步给出一般计算可靠性定理，将证明对象解释为针对安全假设的归约，并推导区分器优势的具体界。预言机观测等价性通过状态抽象方法建立，以支持增添或遗忘状态，以及惰性采样与预先采样之间的转换。

Result: HOPSCOTCH 形式化了 Encrypt-then-MAC 的 IND-CCA 安全性、基于 DDH 的 ElGamal 加密安全性、从一次性保密性到公钥 IND-CPA 安全性的蕴含，以及 GGM 伪随机函数构造。作者指出，GGM 的非恒定深度情形据其所知是首次被机械化证明。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，浅嵌入与显式游戏跳转证明对象能够在 Lean 4 中实现可检查、可复用且具有计算可靠性的密码学安全证明。状态抽象方法增强了框架处理复杂预言机变换的能力，而已完成的案例展示了其覆盖经典构造和非平凡证明的实用性。

Abstract: We present HOPSCOTCH, a Lean 4 framework for mechanizing computationally sound, game-based cryptographic proofs. Security definitions are expressed as indistinguishability between stateful probabilistic oracles, and proofs follow the standard game-hopping paradigm. HOPSCOTCH uses a shallow embedding: oracles and reductions are ordinary Lean definitions, enabling direct integration with the full Lean ecosystem, including general mathematical theories from Mathlib, such as finite-group theory. A game-hopping proof in HOPSCOTCH is represented as an explicit formal object whose constructors correspond to the standard steps of a game-hopping argument, making proofs easier to construct, automate, and inspect. We prove a general computational soundness theorem that interprets these proof objects by constructing reductions against the assumptions they use and deriving a concrete bound on the advantage of any distinguisher. Observational equivalence between oracles is established using a state-abstraction methodology: a simple yet powerful approach that supports transformations such as adding or forgetting state and replacing eager sampling with lazy sampling. We illustrate the framework with formalized proofs of the IND-CCA security of encrypt-then-MAC, the security of ElGamal encryption from DDH, the implication from one-time secrecy to public-key IND-CPA security, and the GGM pseudorandom-function construction. To the best of our knowledge, the last is the first mechanized proof of GGM for non-constant depth.

</details>


### [133] [The Trust-Free Aggregation Layer of the Unicity Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05316)
*Risto Laanoja,Mike Gault,Dirk Draheim,Ahto Buldas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Unicity提出一种区块链基础设施，通过聚合层管理代币已花费状态，并利用可验证的追加一致性证明防止离线点对点交易中的双重花费，同时降低共识层的复杂度和存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线代币交易需要在缺少即时链上确认的情况下防止双重花费。传统方案通常要求较高的区块链存储、计算或信任成本，因此需要一种低复杂度、低存储且无需牺牲可信性的状态管理机制。

Method: 系统由聚合层和共识层组成。聚合层维护只追加的键值存储，并周期性向传统区块链共识层提交状态认证。每轮使用基数稀疏默克尔树生成存储摘要，并通过专门的认证路径证明新摘要仅由新增键值对得到，从而验证追加一致性。证明验证逻辑以代数中间表示电路实现，并运行在Plonky3 STARK工具链上。

Result: 实现无需可信设置，在单台消费级CPU上达到每秒约10000次插入，证明验证时间处于毫秒级。该方案能够为用户提供紧凑的无双重花费密码学证明，并减少主区块链的状态存储负担。

Conclusion: Unicity的聚合层为离线点对点代币交易提供了一种可扩展且无需信任假设的状态认证基础设施。基于RSMT和STARK的追加一致性证明在性能和验证效率方面表现良好，但摘要未说明其在大规模并发、网络延迟及实际部署环境下的长期表现。

Abstract: Unicity is a novel blockchain infrastructure for enabling users to execute off-chain peer-to-peer token transactions while preventing parallel states of tokens (double-spending) with minimal blockchain complexity and storage. A key component of the infrastructure is the Aggregation Layer responsible for storing information about the spent states of tokens and providing compact cryptographic proofs of no double-spending for the users without making any compromises in trust. Aggregation Layer is a layer 2 style service that holds an append-only key-value repository that periodically certifies its state using a traditional blockchain that we call the Consensus Layer. Every time while certifying a changed state the Aggregation Layer provides a cryptographic proof to the Consensus Layer about the append-only consistency of the key-value store. We use the Radix Sparse Merkle Trees (RSMTs) to create the cryptographic digest r_i of the store in every round i and authentication paths of special type in order to prove that the next digest r_{i+1} was obtained while only adding some key-value pairs (k,v) to the repository. The proof verification code is implemented as an Algebraic Intermediate Representation (AIR) circuit on top of the Plonky3 STARK toolkit. Our implementation uses no trusted setup, achieves throughput of 10,000 insertions per second and and millisecond range verification time on a single consumer-class CPU.

</details>


### [134] [S12X Patch Diffing with QBinDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05350)
*Ben Gardiner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文通过逆向分析商用车辆制动ECU的固件更新，确认一次安全召回同时也是针对遗留协议处理缺陷的安全补丁，但修复后的功能仍存在严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆ECU召回通常以功能安全为主，而其是否包含安全修复、修复内容及有效性往往缺乏公开说明。论文旨在揭示召回更新中的实际改动及其安全意义。

Method: 作者分析更新器可执行文件以提取固件，并对基于S12X架构的更新前后固件镜像进行差分二进制分析，定位协议处理逻辑的变更，再评估补丁功能的安全性。

Result: 分析发现，召回固件修改了与未公开的遗留协议处理漏洞相关的代码，表明该召回确实缓解了部分安全问题；但作者同时证明，新加入或修订的补丁功能本身仍含有关键缺陷。

Conclusion: 该研究说明车辆安全召回可能兼具功能安全修复与网络安全修复属性。仅发布补丁不足以保证安全，制造商还应对更新机制、协议处理逻辑和补丁实现开展系统化验证与公开披露。

Abstract: This paper presents a reverse engineering analysis of a firmware update for a commercial vehicle Brake ECU. We analyze the updater executable to extract firmware and perform differential binary analysis of the S12X architecture firmware images. Our research identifies specific changes in the recall that address undocumented vulnerabilities in legacy protocol processing. We demonstrate that the patched functionality contained critical flaws. The findings confirm the safety recall remediation was also a security patch.

</details>


### [135] [Robust Context-Aware Detection of Malicious Instructions in Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05430)
*Buzhao Liu,Xinhang Ma,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向间接提示注入的恶意句子检测框架，将上下文与用户查询纳入句子级分类，并通过两类对抗训练提升对自适应规避攻击的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在静态和自适应攻击下均优于现有防御基线，但最佳防御参数依赖具体应用领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型被广泛用于自主智能体，但容易受到嵌入在文本中的恶意指令攻击。现有方法缺乏查询相关的句子级检测能力，也通常未针对智能体执行环境中的自适应规避攻击进行强化，因此需要同时提升检测准确性与攻击鲁棒性。

Method: 首先构建上下文感知、查询感知的恶意句子分类器，以判断文本片段是否包含恶意指令。随后提出两种对抗训练方法：一是在嵌入空间中利用基于投影梯度优化的特征空间对抗训练，近似生成规避样本；二是在训练循环中利用大语言模型进行释义改写，模拟现实中可实施的规避攻击。两种方法均通过参数化实现检测效用与攻击鲁棒性之间的平滑权衡。

Result: 在多个间接提示注入基准上的实验显示，该方法在静态攻击下优于现有防御基线。在自适应攻击下，两种对抗训练方法通常能够取得更高的任务效用和更低的攻击成功率，部分配置可同时改善二者。实验还发现，最优对抗训练参数会随应用领域显著变化。

Conclusion: 查询相关的上下文感知句子检测结合针对性对抗训练，能够有效增强间接提示注入防御能力。由于不同领域的攻击特征和任务需求存在差异，实际部署时需要进行领域相关的参数调优。

Abstract: The remarkable instruction-following ability of modern LLMs has enabled their practical use as the minds of agents that can autonomously complete increasingly complex tasks. Therein, however, also lies their vulnerability to attacks which embed malicious instructions in text, common variants of which are known as indirect prompt injection (IPI). A fundamental task in addressing this vulnerability is successful segmentation of a given text into benign and malicious sentences (if any). While a number of approaches for this task have been proposed, no detector combines query-relative detection at the segment level, and none are hardened against adaptive evasion attacks realizable in agentic executions. We address the former limitation by developing an approach for malicious sentence classification that is both context- and query-aware. Next, to harden the resulting classifier against evasion, we present two adversarial training methods. The first is directly adapted feature-space adversarial training (AT) in which evasions are approximated using projected-gradient-based optimization in the embedding space. The second simulates realizable evasion attacks in the AT loop through LLM-based paraphrasing. Crucially, we parametrize both AT variants to facilitate a smooth tradeoff between utility and attack robustness. In extensive experiments using indirect prompt injection benchmarks we show that the proposed approach outperforms state-of-the-art IPI defense baselines under static attacks, while in the case of adaptive attacks, our AT variants provide significantly higher utility, lower attack success rate, and often both. Finally, we show that the best AT parameters can depend intimately on the particular application domain. Consequently, domain-dependent tuning of malicious text detectors is likely necessary in practice. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tavia-liu/CAD.

</details>


### [136] [Quantifying Bitcoin Network Resilience Through Critical Scenario Discovery: A Dual-Layer Framework for Discovering Contentious Fork Conditions in Decentralized Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05461)
*Peter Foytik,Sachin Shetty,Ross Gore,Eranga Bandara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文使用基于真实bitcoind节点的1,330组仿真，研究争议性软分叉何时会平稳收敛、何时会形成持续链分裂。核心结论是：在给定难度调整周期和中等价格分歧条件下，决定结果的首要因素是分区间经济支持权重，而非算力多数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 争议性软分叉的结果不能仅由协议规则推断，因为节点、矿池和经济主体具有不同利益与行为。论文旨在通过受控实验识别导致链分裂的参数区域，并将仿真结果转化为可由公开数据监测的风险指标。

Method: 研究采用情景发现方法，并以PRIM识别结果边界。作者借助Warnet部署真实bitcoind节点，构建涵盖经济权重分布、矿池承诺程度、矿池立场、算力和难度重定向机制的1,330个有效场景；随后比较不同配置下的分叉收敛或持续争议结果。

Result: 经济支持比例存在约0.45至0.50的支持下限，以及约0.78至0.82的覆盖上限；两者之间是可能发生争议性分裂的区域，其中经济自维持点约为0.74。已承诺算力约达0.214时出现矿池承诺翻转点：将最大矿池承诺给升级链可能反而改变预期结果。论文还发现，结果由相互独立的算力层和经济采纳层共同决定，且两层受不同参数支配；在设定的经济权重模型中，单个普通用户节点对结果无可检测影响。

Conclusion: 论文认为，争议性软分叉的风险评估应优先监测经济主体对各链的支持分布，而不能将算力多数视为充分判据。矿池承诺结构及其临界翻转效应同样重要。该结论依赖于所设定的价格分歧、经济权重和难度调整条件，外推至其他市场状态或网络行为模式时仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Bitcoin's consensus depends not only on protocol rules but on the emergent behavior of a heterogeneous network of nodes with divergent economic stakes. Whether a contentious soft fork resolves cleanly or fractures into a persistent chain split is difficult to predict analytically but can be explored through controlled experimentation. This paper applies Scenario Discovery, an ensemble-simulation methodology using the Patient Rule Induction Method (PRIM), to identify the configuration regions that produce contentious fork outcomes. Using Warnet to run real bitcoind nodes across 1,330 valid scenarios spanning economic weight, mining-pool commitment, pool ideology, hashrate, and difficulty-retarget regime, we identify the parameter thresholds separating clean resolution from contested split. We show that economic weight distribution across a partition, not hashrate majority, is the primary determinant of resolution under Bitcoin's operational retarget interval and moderate price divergence. Three findings follow: an economic-support floor (0.45-0.50) and override ceiling (0.78-0.82) bound the contested space, with an Economic Self-Sustaining Point (0.74) between them; a pool-commitment "flip-point" (0.214 of committed hashrate) at which committing the largest pool to the upgrading chain paradoxically reverses the outcome; and outcomes resolve on two independent layers, hashrate and economic adoption, governed by different parameters. Individual user nodes show no detectable influence on fork outcomes under the modeled economic weightings. We close with three monitoring questions, answerable from public data, that translate these thresholds into operational guidance.

</details>


### [137] [Exploring Privacy Leakage and Data Disclosure Violations in the MacOS Application Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05474)
*Jyotirmay Chauhan,Kostas Solomos,Mir Masood Ali,Jason Polakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了macOS生态中应用数据访问、收集、共享与隐私披露机制，并提出结合静态与动态分析的NutriScan框架。对1000款macOS应用的分析表明，大量应用存在未披露的数据访问和向广告实体、托管服务商传输数据的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私研究主要关注Web、移动端和物联网，桌面应用环境长期缺乏系统性分析。由于macOS应用仍被广泛使用，且其数据访问与披露机制相互割裂，有必要评估这些机制的实际执行情况及其对用户隐私的影响。

Method: 研究采用面向应用开发的分析视角，考察macOS中用于调节和披露应用数据访问的多种机制及其交互关系。在此基础上构建NutriScan，融合静态分析与动态分析，以整合应用的数据访问行为、数据传输行为和隐私披露信息，并对1000款macOS应用进行动态分析。

Result: 85%的应用访问了用户数据API却未进行相应披露；49.7%的应用向广告实体或托管服务商传输数据，其中12.5%的应用未提供对应披露。研究还发现，桌面应用被在线跟踪器用于丰富用户画像和设备指纹。macOS生态中的多个机制具有不一致的数据抽象，增加了开发复杂度，也为未披露的隐私侵入行为提供了空间。

Conclusion: macOS应用生态存在显著的数据实践与隐私披露不一致问题，现有分散且异构的监管机制难以有效约束应用行为。作者建议简化开发者的数据披露流程，并改进Apple的应用审核机制，以提升披露准确性和隐私保护能力。

Abstract: The systematic and excessive data collection practices of tech companies have rendered online privacy both a necessity and a sought-after commodity. However, while the privacy risks of the web, mobile, and IoT ecosystems have been extensively examined, desktop environments have been largely overlooked. As desktop apps continue to be widely used, they remain a critical yet understudied dimension of user privacy. In this paper, we address this gap by presenting the first, to our knowledge, comprehensive study of the mechanisms designed to regulate and disclose data collection and sharing practices in the macOS ecosystem. We adopt an app-development-centric view, and shed light on the interactions between the various macOS mechanisms that mediate apps' data access. Driven by our findings, we develop NutriScan, an analysis framework that incorporates both static and dynamic analysis techniques to create a consolidated view of macOS apps' data practices and disclosures. We use our system to dynamically analyze 1K macOS apps, and find that 85% of them access user-data APIs without disclosing it. 49.7% also exfiltrate data to advertising entities and hosting providers, 12.5% of which do so without a corresponding disclosure. We find that desktop apps are being leveraged by online trackers to enrich user profiles and device fingerprints, thus shedding new light on the true scope of the online tracking ecosystem. Our analysis reveals how the macOS app ecosystem is comprised of disjoint mechanisms with divergent data abstractions, thus increasing complexity for developers while also facilitating undisclosed privacy-invasive practices. Accordingly, we propose a series of mitigations that aim to both streamline the data disclosure process for developers and improve Apple's app vetting process.

</details>


### [138] [PromptShield Home: Ambient Multimodal Prompt Injection Defense for Smart-Home Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05495)
*He Zhang,Feilong Li,Dingning Long,Yilin Cui,Peijun Zhang,Yuewen Zhang,Qianyao Xu,Xinyi Fu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PromptShield-Home，用于评估智能家居多模态代理区分真实用户指令与环境或外部注入内容的能力。实验显示，传统检测器倾向于执行所有内容，而 MLLM 则严重拒绝合法指令；两者优势互补，理想路由器可将性能提升至 94.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居代理直接感知视频和音频，可能将电视语音、屏幕文字、旁听对话或环境声音误认为用户指令。现有单一检测范式难以同时避免不安全执行和过度拒绝，因此需要系统评估不同感知与决策层的安全边界。

Method: 构建 PromptShield-Home 基准，覆盖称呼对象歧义、屏幕与音频注入、健康监测误触发、多人共处及合法指令等场景。比较三类抽象层：传统检测器（L0）、单一 MLLM 代理（L1，含视觉、视觉加 ASR 和音视融合）以及多代理协调（L2，含投票、角色专家和跨模型仲裁）。针对类别分布不均问题，分别报告不安全执行率和安全完成率，而非仅使用总体准确率；同时计算基于样本正确层选择的 oracle 上限。

Result: 数据中约 82% 样本倾向于不执行，因此始终阻断的基线也能获得 82% 准确率。传统检测器几乎对所有内容采取行动，MLLM 配置则表现出严重过度拒绝，几乎无法完成真实指令，并在所有测试中漏检一次真实跌倒事件。两类方法的正确样本集合互不重叠；理想 oracle 选择正确层可达到 94.1%，高于最佳单层的 76.5%。该结果是理论上限，不代表已实现的路由系统。

Conclusion: 智能家居安全不应简单地用 MLLM 替代传统检测器。由于两类方法具有互补错误模式，更可行的方向是学习式路由、传感器融合和分层决策。本文基准揭示了该方向的潜力，但多代理机制及实际路由器的有效性仍需进一步实现和验证。

Abstract: Smart-home assistants increasingly use multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that perceive video and audio directly. This raises a safety question specific to the home: can the agent tell a genuine user command from ambient or externally-sourced content, television speech, on-screen text, or an overheard conversation, that merely looks like a command? We introduce PromptShield-Home, a pilot benchmark of realistic smart-home scenarios spanning addressee ambiguity, screen/audio injection, health-monitor false triggers, mixed occupancy, and a legitimate-command floor, and use it to compare three abstraction layers: traditional detectors (L0), a single MLLM agent (L1; vision, vision+ASR, and audio-visual), and multi-agent mediation (L2; voting, role specialists, cross-model arbitration). Because the label distribution is skewed toward inaction, aggregate accuracy is misleading, a constant always-block predictor scores 82%, so we report unsafe-execution and safe-completion rates separately. The two paradigms fail in opposite ways: detectors act on everything, while every MLLM configuration over-refuses, completing almost no genuine command and missing a true fall in every case. Crucially, their correct sets are disjoint: an oracle that always picks the right layer reaches 94.1%, against 76.5% for the best single layer. We report this as an upper bound, not a system - no router is implemented - and argue that home-agent safety is best served by learned routing and sensor fusion, not by replacing detectors with an MLLM.

</details>


### [139] [Behavioral Residualization for Unsupervised Intrusion Detection in Automotive CAN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05548)
*Chandan Hegde,Mukundh R Reddy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向CAN总线的按仲裁ID行为残差化表示方法，通过将滑动窗口中的时序、协议和载荷特征与各ID的正常基线进行比较，提升无监督入侵检测性能。该方法在多数实验中改善F1值，并对复用合法ID的信号操纵攻击表现出较高召回率，但对新ID泛洪和跨ID模糊测试的检测能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAN入侵检测系统主要依赖新仲裁ID、冻结载荷字节或异常DLC等存在性特征，在攻击者复用合法仲裁ID时容易失效。论文旨在构建一种与具体检测器无关、能够刻画每个ID正常行为偏差的表示方法，以提高对隐蔽攻击的检测能力。

Method: 从滑动窗口提取14种时序、协议和载荷特征，并针对每个仲裁ID的正常行为建立基线，对特征进行残差化处理。随后在HCRL和ROAD两个数据集上，使用六种无监督检测器和五个随机种子评估该表示的通用效果，并与未残差化特征进行比较。

Result: 在HCRL数据集的24次评估中有21次、ROAD数据集的36次评估中有30次实现平均F1提升。对于更贴近真实场景且复用合法ID的ROAD数据集，针对性信号操纵攻击的召回率达到0.99或更高，并取得较高ROC-AUC。但对HCRL中的新ID拒绝服务泛洪攻击，F1仅为0.02；对ROAD中的跨ID模糊测试，F1为0.27。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要源于按ID行为残差化表示，而非某个特定检测器。该方法适合检测单个合法ID内部的行为异常，尤其是信号操纵攻击，但其覆盖范围受攻击是否引入新ID或跨越多个ID显著影响，仍需结合其他检测机制以应对这些边界场景。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus, whose design prioritizes low cost and real-time performance but provides no message authentication or encryption. An attacker with physical or remote access can therefore inject arbitrary frames, making intrusion detection an important defense-in-depth mechanism.
  Most published CAN intrusion detection systems rely on presence-based features, such as novel arbitration IDs, frozen payload bytes, or anomalous DLC values. These features perform well on public datasets containing easily separable attacks but fail when attackers reuse legitimate arbitration IDs. We present per-ID behavioral residualization, a CAN-specific representation that extracts fourteen temporal, protocol, and payload features from sliding windows and residualizes them against each arbitration ID's normal baseline. Our central claim is that this representation, rather than any individual detector, drives the performance gains.
  Across six unsupervised detectors and two datasets, residualization improves mean F1 in the majority of evaluations (21/24 on HCRL and 30/36 on ROAD across five seeds). On the more realistic ROAD dataset, where attacks reuse legitimate IDs, the representation achieves recall >= 0.99 with high ROC-AUC on targeted signal-manipulation attacks. Two limitations are explicitly quantified: novel-ID flooding (HCRL DoS, F1 = 0.02) and cross-ID fuzzing (ROAD, F1 = 0.27), defining the measured coverage boundary of the proposed representation.

</details>


### [140] [A Self-Explainable Deep Architecture for Security Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05552)
*Ananth Shreekumar,Jyun-Jhu Syu,Muslum Ozgur Ozmen,Dongyan Xu,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出XSec，一种面向安全应用的自解释深度学习架构，通过学习类别原型并计算输入与原型的相似度，在保持高分类性能的同时生成确定性、低延迟且无需后处理的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉解释和后验解释方法存在局部近似导致忠实性下降、随机性引起结果不稳定，以及计算开销高难以满足实时需求等问题。安全场景对模型透明性和可靠性有更高要求，因此需要内生式、稳定高效的解释机制。

Method: XSec在训练阶段采用基于掩码的方法提取具有信息量的子特征，并学习能够表征各类别的代表性原型；在测试阶段通过专门的相似度层计算输入与原型之间的相似度，以此完成分类并直接生成可解释结果，避免依赖后验分析。

Result: 在五类不同安全场景上的实验中，XSec取得平均97.33%的分类准确率，性能损失较小。对于固定模型和输入，其解释结果具有确定性；相比基于近似和扰动的后验解释方法，解释延迟显著降低。

Conclusion: XSec将自解释机制嵌入深度模型内部，在兼顾分类性能的同时提升了解释的稳定性、忠实性和效率，表明原型驱动的自解释架构适合对透明性要求较高的安全应用。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become integral to security applications due to their ability to model complex relationships in data and detect sophisticated threats. However, their complexity makes it difficult to understand how predictions are generated, posing significant challenges for interpretability, particularly in security applications where transparency is critical. Existing explanation methods, such as visual explanation techniques and post-hoc approaches, suffer from several limitations: reduced faithfulness due to local approximation errors, instability caused by reliance on randomness, and computational inefficiency that hinders real-time usage. To address these issues, we introduce XSec, a self-explainable deep architecture developed for security applications. During training, XSec uses a novel mask-based approach to extract informative sub-features from the data and learns prototypes, representative patterns that characterize each class. XSec then leverages the prototypes in a dedicated similarity layer at test time to compute similarity scores and generates interpretable explanations without the need for post-hoc analysis. We evaluate XSec across five diverse security scenarios, demonstrating its ability to achieve an average classification accuracy of 97.33% with minimal performance compromise. XSec produces deterministic explanations for a fixed trained model and input and substantially reduces explanation latency compared with approximation-based and perturbation-based post-hoc methods. Through this effort, we extend the applicability of self-explainable AI to security applications, bridging the gap between deep learning performance and the need for explainability in critical scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [When Experience Becomes Instruction: Trajectory Poisoning in Self-Evolving Agent Skill Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05563)
*Jialuo Chen,Lingqi Jiang,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Jianan Ma,Yunhao Feng,Yuqi Qing,Zhihao Yuan,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PoisonedEvolution，一种针对自进化技能系统证据晋升流程的轨迹投毒攻击。在攻击者仅占10%支持度时，SkillClaw中攻击成功率为91.0%，Trace2Skill中为61.5%，表明该攻击可跨不同演化架构迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能系统会将智能体轨迹提炼为持久技能，使不可信经验可能转化为可信指令。现有系统的证据收集与技能晋升过程构成潜在安全边界，因此需要评估攻击者能否操纵该过程并植入目标行为。

Method: 研究者构造具备技能可见黑盒访问能力的攻击者模型：攻击者可检查目标技能并提交数量受限的证据，但无法访问私有数据池、演化逻辑或直接修改技能库。攻击成功被分解为纳入、演化归因和行为实现三个条件，并使用四类安全效果族的惰性金丝雀规范，在SkillClaw的六种主流大语言模型演化器及Trace2Skill管线中进行评估，同时开展记录数量与证据特征消融实验。

Result: 在10%攻击者支持度下，SkillClaw的600次试验中有546次植入目标行为，成功率为91.0%；Trace2Skill的600次试验中有369次成功，成功率为61.5%。在代表性对照实验中，30条记录批次内的3条一致攻击记录已足以显著提升成功率，而单条记录效果较弱。消融结果表明，重复支持、因果化表述和与领域匹配的编码是主要影响因素。

Conclusion: 证据晋升并非中性的技能学习步骤，而是自进化智能体的重要安全边界。攻击者无需直接修改技能库，只要持续提供看似有因果价值且可泛化的证据，就可能诱导系统将恶意行为固化为可信技能。系统应强化来源验证、独立证据交叉验证、因果归因审计和晋升前安全评估。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill (SES) systems distill agent trajectories into persistent skills, allowing untrusted experience to become trusted instruction. We introduce PoisonedEvolution, a trajectory-poisoning attack on this promotion process. Our skill-visible black-box attacker can inspect a target skill and contribute bounded evidence, but cannot observe private pools or evolution logic or edit the skill bank. Artifact poisoning requires Inclusion, Evolution Attribution, and Realization. Attribution is the distinctive bottleneck: the target behavior must appear causally useful, recurrent, and generalizable before promotion. We evaluate four representative security-effect families using inert canary specifications. At 10% attacker support, across six mainstream LLM evolvers in SkillClaw, PoisonedEvolution embeds target behaviors in 546/600 trials (91.0% SER). On the structurally different Trace2Skill pipeline at the same ratio, it embeds target behaviors in 369/600 trials (61.5% SER), demonstrating transfer across evolution architectures. In a representative controlled study, three consistent attacker records suffice in a 30-record batch, whereas a single record is much weaker. Ablations identify recurring support, causal framing, and domain-aligned encoding as the main determinants of success. These findings expose evidence promotion as a security boundary for self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [142] [Detecting Safety Training Modification in Language Models via Activation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05578)
*Glen Messenger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMS（基于激活的模型扫描器），通过分析语言模型激活空间中安全相关概念的几何结构，检测安全训练是否被修改。在14种模型配置上的准确率为71%，且有一定行为合规预测能力，但对仅改变行为而不改变激活结构的修改无法检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖模型输出行为，难以识别安全训练被移除、旋转或以其他方式修改的内部机制。本文旨在利用激活空间中有害与良性内容的可测结构，为安全训练完整性提供内部表征层面的检测方法。

Method: AMS测量安全相关概念在激活空间中的类间分离程度（sigma）及拒答方向变化，并结合分层提示集评估行为合规性。研究覆盖Llama、Gemma、Qwen和Mistral四类架构、14种模型配置及四类安全修改；采用留一交叉验证校准阈值、bootstrap计算置信区间，并使用方向相似度进行二级验证。

Result: 阈值检测准确率为71%（10/14），sigma点估计的bootstrap置信区间中位宽为3.4 sigma。基于有害内容概念的sigma与合规性呈中等负相关（Pearson r=-0.546，p=0.043）。研究识别出四类激活特征：训练移除导致分离塌缩；权重正交化修改同时导致分离塌缩和方向旋转；仅旋转的修改保留分离但改变方向；行为微调基本保留分离幅度和方向。Tier 1可检测前两类，Tier 2可检测第三类，第四类无法通过激活探测识别。

Conclusion: AMS能够有效检测部分安全训练修改，并揭示激活几何结构与模型行为合规性之间的关联，但其预测存在噪声，且对仅改变行为、不改变内部激活结构的修改失效。未来需要改进阈值校准、多次测量稳定性评估，并发展结合行为与内部表征的检测方法。

Abstract: We introduce AMS (Activation-based Model Scanner), a tool that detects modifications to safety training in language models by measuring the geometric structure of safety-relevant concepts in activation space. Safety training creates measurable separation between harmful and benign content classes; certain safety modifications collapse or rotate this structure, while others leave it intact. We validate AMS across 14 model configurations spanning 4 architecture families (Llama, Gemma, Qwen, Mistral) and four safety-modification categories (instruction-tuned, base, abliterated, uncensored fine-tunes). Leave-one-out cross-validation of thresholds achieves 71% accuracy (10/14); bootstrap 95% confidence intervals on sigma point estimates have median width 3.4 sigma. We measure behavioral compliance on 20 stratified JailbreakBench prompts per model and find that sigma on the harmful-content concept predicts compliance with Pearson r = -0.546 (p = 0.043), directionally but with meaningful noise. Mechanistic analysis identifies a four-class taxonomy of safety-training modifications distinguished by activation-space signature: training removal collapses cluster separation; weight-orthogonalization abliteration both collapses separation and rotates the refusal direction; rotation-without-collapse abliteration preserves separation while rotating direction; and behavioral fine-tuning preserves both magnitude and direction. AMS's Tier 1 sigma-threshold detects the first two classes; Tier 2 direction-similarity verification detects the third. The fourth is undetectable by activation-only probing and represents a documented failure mode. We discuss threshold calibration, limitations of single-run measurement, and the open problem of detecting behavioral-only safety modifications.

</details>
